# Daily video prompt pack — 17/11/2026 · Goat day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំមមែ)

Lucky signs: Goat, Rabbit, Pig · Careful: Ox
Day element: Yin Wood 🌿 (Yi-wei day; ធាតុឈើ) · every action scene today carries Wood magic: fresh green leaves, glowing jade vines and young bamboo shoots that sprout and spiral through the air.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 17/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Goat's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)

It stands in a courtyard of a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, beside two carved stone lions and a row of glowing oil lamps, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the goat, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

Two-shot of Chanda and the goat together on the moonlit temple terrace, both clearly in frame. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, faces the camera, smiling and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements. ## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Goat, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Goat as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Goat's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Goat and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Goat? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Goat, people may describe you as gentle, creative, and compassionate. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា សុភាពរាបសា ច្នៃប្រឌិត និងអាណិតអាសូរ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: gentle, creative, and compassionate.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ សុភាពរាបសា ច្នៃប្រឌិត និងអាណិតអាសូរ។

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Goat? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Tuesday, 17 November 2026, is a Goat day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Goat. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Goat are gentle, creative, and compassionate. Goats have a strong artistic sensibility and care deeply for others.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Goat? Your luck is strong today. Stay gentle, kind and peaceful. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: A favourable day for steady income. Save a small part of today's earnings before treating yourself.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is green. Wear something green, or carry a small green item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 5 to 7 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the east. When you start something new, face the east, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 225. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 7, 27, 225!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Goat's lucky days of the week are Friday and Sunday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive or overly dependent on others. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: green. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 225.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំពីរ លេខម្ភៃប្រាំពីរ និងលេខពីររយម្ភៃប្រាំ។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

### Subtitles – Khmer (2026-11-17-180s.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,392
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែមែនទេ?

2
00:00:02,442 --> 00:00:07,392
នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

3
00:00:07,442 --> 00:00:09,950
ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,642
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃអង្គារ ទី 17 ខែវិច្ឆិកា

5
00:00:12,692 --> 00:00:14,796
ឆ្នាំ 2026 គឺជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំមមែ។

6
00:00:14,846 --> 00:00:18,027
នេះជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ។

7
00:00:18,077 --> 00:00:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក!

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,124
ពពែ ស្លូតបូត និងចិត្តល្អ។

9
00:00:22,174 --> 00:00:24,211
ឆ្នាំមមែតំណាងឲ្យសន្តិភាព

10
00:00:24,261 --> 00:00:26,385
ភាពសុខដុម និងចិត្តមេត្តា។

11
00:00:26,435 --> 00:00:29,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថាមពលល្អទាំងនេះ កំពុងនៅជាមួយអ្នក។

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,907
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ជាមនុស្សសុភាពរាបសា

13
00:00:32,957 --> 00:00:35,080
ច្នៃប្រឌិត និងអាណិតអាសូរ។

14
00:00:35,130 --> 00:00:37,341
មមែមានអារម្មណ៍សិល្បៈខ្លាំង

15
00:00:37,391 --> 00:00:39,950
និងយកចិត្តទុកដាក់ចំពោះអ្នកដទៃ។

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,172
សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ

17
00:00:42,222 --> 00:00:44,172
សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។

18
00:00:44,222 --> 00:00:46,839
សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

19
00:00:46,889 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,450
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖

21
00:00:52,500 --> 00:00:56,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

22
00:00:57,000 --> 00:00:59,950
និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,987
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន

24
00:01:02,037 --> 00:01:05,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

25
00:01:05,556 --> 00:01:08,006
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត

26
00:01:08,056 --> 00:01:09,950
និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

27
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:14,293
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ចំណូលនឹងនរ។

28
00:01:14,343 --> 00:01:18,031
សូមសន្សំប្រាក់ចំណូលថ្ងៃនេះមួយចំណែកតូច

29
00:01:18,081 --> 00:01:19,950
មុននឹងចាយលើខ្លួនឯង។

30
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,932
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌បៃតង។

31
00:01:22,982 --> 00:01:26,178
សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌បៃតង ឬយករបស់ពណ៌បៃតងមួយ

32
00:01:26,228 --> 00:01:29,950
ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

33
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,357
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

34
00:01:32,407 --> 00:01:34,672
គឺម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

35
00:01:34,722 --> 00:01:37,357
ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ

36
00:01:37,407 --> 00:01:39,950
ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

37
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,901
ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសខាងកើត។

38
00:01:42,951 --> 00:01:46,589
ពេលចាប់ផ្ដើមធ្វើអ្វីថ្មី សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសខាងកើត

39
00:01:46,639 --> 00:01:49,950
ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

40
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,677
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 27 និងលេខ 225។

41
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់

42
00:01:57,364 --> 00:01:59,950
ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ 7 27 225!

43
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,103
ថ្ងៃសំណាងប្រចាំសប្ដាហ៍ របស់ឆ្នាំមមែ

44
00:02:03,153 --> 00:02:05,536
គឺថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ។

45
00:02:05,586 --> 00:02:07,247
សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ

46
00:02:07,297 --> 00:02:09,950
សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

47
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,246
សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ

48
00:02:12,296 --> 00:02:14,913
ជួនកាលស្ទាក់ស្ទើរក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត

49
00:02:14,963 --> 00:02:16,543
ឬពឹងផ្អែកលើអ្នកដទៃពេក។

50
00:02:16,593 --> 00:02:18,320
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព

51
00:02:18,370 --> 00:02:19,950
និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

52
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

53
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

54
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

55
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

56
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

57
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

58
00:02:37,712 --> 00:02:39,950
ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

59
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,644
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

60
00:02:41,694 --> 00:02:43,095
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

61
00:02:43,145 --> 00:02:45,595
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

62
00:02:45,645 --> 00:02:47,208
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

63
00:02:47,258 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 27 និងលេខ 225។

64
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

65
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

66
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

67
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

68
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-17-180s.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,611
Born in the Year of the Goat?

2
00:00:02,661 --> 00:00:04,721
Here's your lucky color

3
00:00:04,771 --> 00:00:07,656
best time, and numbers for today

4
00:00:07,706 --> 00:00:09,950
plus one gentle reminder.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,393
Today, Tuesday, 17 November 2026

6
00:00:12,443 --> 00:00:13,461
is a Goat day.

7
00:00:13,511 --> 00:00:16,133
It is a Great Day for everyone born

8
00:00:16,183 --> 00:00:17,965
in the Year of the Goat.

9
00:00:18,015 --> 00:00:19,950
This is your day to shine!

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,314
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes

11
00:00:23,364 --> 00:00:26,586
peace, harmony, and a caring heart.

12
00:00:26,636 --> 00:00:29,950
Today, this good energy is with you.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,415
People born in the Year of the Goat

14
00:00:32,465 --> 00:00:35,232
are gentle, creative, and compassionate.

15
00:00:35,282 --> 00:00:38,049
Goats have a strong artistic sensibility

16
00:00:38,099 --> 00:00:39,950
and care deeply for others.

17
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,740
Born in the Year of the Goat?

18
00:00:41,790 --> 00:00:43,345
Your luck is strong today.

19
00:00:43,395 --> 00:00:45,259
Stay gentle, kind and peaceful.

20
00:00:45,309 --> 00:00:47,357
Say yes to good chances, and share

21
00:00:47,407 --> 00:00:49,950
your happiness with the people around you.

22
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,394
Love and family today:

23
00:00:52,444 --> 00:00:56,506
A good day to reach out, share a meal

24
00:00:56,556 --> 00:00:59,950
and show your affection openly.

25
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,969
Work today: Customers

26
00:01:02,019 --> 00:01:05,238
and coworkers respond well to you.

27
00:01:05,288 --> 00:01:08,315
A good day to present your ideas

28
00:01:08,365 --> 00:01:09,950
and close a deal.

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,611
Money today: A favourable day

30
00:01:12,661 --> 00:01:14,262
for steady income.

31
00:01:14,312 --> 00:01:16,831
Save a small part of today's

32
00:01:16,881 --> 00:01:19,950
earnings before treating yourself.

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,807
Your lucky color today is green.

34
00:01:22,857 --> 00:01:24,593
Wear something green

35
00:01:24,643 --> 00:01:27,807
or carry a small green item with you

36
00:01:27,857 --> 00:01:29,950
to keep good luck close.

37
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,689
Your best time today

38
00:01:31,739 --> 00:01:34,298
is from 5 to 7 in the morning.

39
00:01:34,348 --> 00:01:36,993
It is a good time for important

40
00:01:37,043 --> 00:01:39,950
plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

41
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:43,045
Your lucky direction today is the east.

42
00:01:43,095 --> 00:01:45,267
When you start something new

43
00:01:45,317 --> 00:01:47,728
face the east, and walk forward

44
00:01:47,778 --> 00:01:49,950
with a calm and happy heart.

45
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,350
For lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 225.

46
00:01:53,400 --> 00:01:56,450
Comment your lucky number below

47
00:01:56,500 --> 00:01:59,950
and let's say it again: 7, 27, 225!

48
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,217
The Goat's lucky days of the week

49
00:02:03,267 --> 00:02:05,396
are Friday and Sunday.

50
00:02:05,446 --> 00:02:08,069
Keep them in mind for plans

51
00:02:08,119 --> 00:02:09,950
that matter to you.

52
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,005
A gentle reminder: people born

53
00:02:12,055 --> 00:02:14,813
in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive

54
00:02:14,863 --> 00:02:16,868
or overly dependent on others.

55
00:02:16,918 --> 00:02:18,238
Today, stay balanced

56
00:02:18,288 --> 00:02:19,950
and listen to your heart.

57
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,080
Make a small wish today

58
00:02:22,130 --> 00:02:25,135
and take one kind step toward it.

59
00:02:25,185 --> 00:02:27,172
A peaceful heart helps

60
00:02:27,222 --> 00:02:29,950
good luck find its way to you.

61
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,470
Before the day ends

62
00:02:31,520 --> 00:02:34,430
take a quiet moment to say thank you.

63
00:02:34,480 --> 00:02:36,750
Thank the people who help you

64
00:02:36,800 --> 00:02:39,950
and thank yourself for trying your best.

65
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,994
Let's remember today's luck.

66
00:02:42,044 --> 00:02:43,381
Lucky color: green.

67
00:02:43,431 --> 00:02:46,154
Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning.
```

The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Goat and the Lost Lotus Seed" (ពពែមាស និងគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden goat and Kong the little frog must find the lost seed before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Kong once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Goat picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Kong and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Kong the little frog (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Kong, a tiny, lifelike magical green-and-gold frog with bright, expressive eyes, a wide happy smile and a little lotus-leaf hat, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

An original animated bedtime story starring the Goat, with today's lucky color, direction and numbers in the plot. For families, children and zodiac fans who enjoy gentle stories.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Story question

2. Mystery
   English: On the night of the full moon, something at the temple went wrong. Only one little hero could fix it.

### Thumbnail prompt – YouTube (16:9)
Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

With the words drawn in (English):
Eye-catching YouTube thumbnail, horizontal 16:9, 3840 x 2160. Photorealistic still from a high-budget fantasy epic blending Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: real human faces with natural skin, lifelike zodiac animals in engraved ceremonial gold armor with real metallic reflections, very expressive faces and eyes. Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Bright, high-contrast, rich colors (imperial gold, deep navy, ruby red) that stand out on a small phone screen; strong golden rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). ### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures, holding a glowing golden storybook as if about to begin a bedtime story. Behind her, @Goat (the Goat guardian with their goat) glows softly on its lotus pedestal; the zodiac characters look around curiously but do not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the goat is the saved Flow character @Goat. ## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden goat find the lost seed before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the lotus's magic seed was gone. Without it, the lotus could not bloom, and the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden goat. Today was a Goat day, and the goat felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little frog named Kong came hopping over. "Little Goat," he said, "only you can find the lost seed. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Kong smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To bring back the lotus seed, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the goat, and it set off on its adventure, with Kong right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Goat are gentle, creative, and compassionate. Goats have a strong artistic sensibility and care deeply for others.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was green, the goat's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The goat tied a green ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Kong, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the east, the goat's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But a wide river blocked the path, with only small stepping stones to cross it. The goat stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive or overly dependent on others.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Kong came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Stone by stone, the goat hopped across the river. Each stone glowed gold under its feet. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Kong. For the Goat today, that is from 5 to 7 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the goat rang the golden bells 7 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The goat climbed 27 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found the magic lotus seed, glowing like a tiny sun. It was the heart of the giant lotus!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the seed, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Friday and Sunday shone brightest, the Goat's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the seed carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Kong right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The goat placed the seed in the giant lotus. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! The lotus opened in a burst of golden light!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The giant lotus bloomed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The goat felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Goat, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: green. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 225.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Kong fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

### Khmer
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
តើពពែមាសតូចមួយ អាចរកគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់ មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ? កាលពីព្រេងនាយ នៅក្បែរប្រាសាទបុរាណមួយ ក្នុងប្រទេសកម្ពុជា រឿងរបស់យើងចាប់ផ្ដើម។

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ គ្រាប់វេទមន្តរបស់ផ្កាឈូក បានបាត់។ បើគ្មានវា ផ្កាឈូកមិនអាចរីកបាន ហើយពិធីបុណ្យក៏មិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានដែរ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក ហើយមានពពែមាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំមមែ ហើយពពែមានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
កង្កែបតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះគង់ លោតតម្រង់មក។ គង់និយាយថា "ពពែតូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចរកគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ពពែ ស្លូតបូត និងចិត្តល្អ។ ឆ្នាំមមែតំណាងឲ្យសន្តិភាព ភាពសុខដុម និងចិត្តមេត្តា។ គង់ញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ដើម្បីនាំគ្រាប់ឈូកត្រឡប់មកវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
ពពែនិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ជាមនុស្សសុភាពរាបសា ច្នៃប្រឌិត និងអាណិតអាសូរ។ មមែមានអារម្មណ៍សិល្បៈខ្លាំង និងយកចិត្តទុកដាក់ចំពោះអ្នកដទៃ។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។ មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។ វាគឺពណ៌បៃតង ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ពពែថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ពពែចងខ្សែបូពណ៌បៃតង ជុំវិញក។ គង់និយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។"

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។ ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។ វាចង្អុលទៅទិសខាងកើត ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ពពែ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ប៉ុន្តែទន្លេធំមួយ បាំងផ្លូវ ហើយមានតែដុំថ្មតូចៗ សម្រាប់ឈានឆ្លង។ ពពែឈប់។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ជួនកាលស្ទាក់ស្ទើរក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត ឬពឹងផ្អែកលើអ្នកដទៃពេក។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
គង់មកជិត ហើយញញឹម។ គង់និយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។ ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។ អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។"

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ពពែលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។ ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
គង់និយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ពពែថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពពែវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនប្រាំពីរ ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ពពែឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនម្ភៃប្រាំពីរ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រាប់ឈូកវេទមន្ត ភ្លឺដូចព្រះអាទិត្យតូចមួយ។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគ្រាប់ឈូក មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមែ។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រាប់ឈូកយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពពែដាក់គ្រាប់ឈូក ចូលក្នុងផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ផ្កាឈូករីក ដោយពន្លឺមាសចែងចាំង!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ រីកម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ពពែមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំពីរ លេខម្ភៃប្រាំពីរ និងលេខពីររយម្ភៃប្រាំ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)

```
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

98
00:04:06,667 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

99
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

100
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

101
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
ពពែមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

102
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

103
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

104
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

105
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

106
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,644
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

107
00:04:31,694 --> 00:04:33,095
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

108
00:04:33,145 --> 00:04:35,595
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

109
00:04:35,645 --> 00:04:37,208
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

110
00:04:37,258 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 7 លេខ 27 និងលេខ 225។

111
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,155
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

112
00:04:44,205 --> 00:04:47,450
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់

113
00:04:47,500 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

114
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

115
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

116
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

117
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

118
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-17-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,728
Can one little golden goat find the lost

2
00:00:02,778 --> 00:00:04,950
seed before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,000 --> 00:00:07,867
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,917 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,306
But this morning

6
00:00:11,356 --> 00:00:14,018
the lotus's magic seed was gone.

7
00:00:14,068 --> 00:00:17,153
Without it, the lotus could not bloom

8
00:00:17,203 --> 00:00:19,950
and the festival could not begin.

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,900
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

10
00:00:22,950 --> 00:00:25,921
opened, and out came a little golden goat.

11
00:00:25,971 --> 00:00:27,360
Today was a Goat day

12
00:00:27,410 --> 00:00:29,950
and the goat felt something special.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,507
A little frog named

14
00:00:31,557 --> 00:00:33,393
Kong came hopping over.

15
00:00:33,443 --> 00:00:35,196
"Little Goat," he said

16
00:00:35,246 --> 00:00:37,901
"only you can find the lost seed.

17
00:00:37,951 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

18
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,807
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes

19
00:00:42,857 --> 00:00:45,585
peace, harmony, and a caring heart.

20
00:00:45,635 --> 00:00:46,537
Kong smiled.

21
00:00:46,587 --> 00:00:48,125
That was exactly why

22
00:00:48,175 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

23
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,172
To bring back the lotus seed

24
00:00:52,222 --> 00:00:54,394
the little hero needed three

25
00:00:54,444 --> 00:00:56,934
lucky treasures: its lucky color

26
00:00:56,984 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

27
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,926
"Let's go!" said the goat

28
00:01:02,976 --> 00:01:06,617
and it set off on its adventure

29
00:01:06,667 --> 00:01:09,950
with Kong right by its side.

30
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,415
People born in the Year of the Goat

31
00:01:12,465 --> 00:01:15,232
are gentle, creative, and compassionate.

32
00:01:15,282 --> 00:01:18,049
Goats have a strong artistic sensibility

33
00:01:18,099 --> 00:01:19,950
and care deeply for others.

34
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,223
The first treasure was waiting

35
00:01:22,273 --> 00:01:24,874
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

36
00:01:24,924 --> 00:01:26,768
Only one shone brightest.

37
00:01:26,818 --> 00:01:28,586
It was green, the goat's

38
00:01:28,636 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

39
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,808
The goat tied a green

40
00:01:31,858 --> 00:01:33,844
ribbon around its neck.

41
00:01:33,894 --> 00:01:36,853
"Wear your lucky color," said Kong

42
00:01:36,903 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

43
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,555
Next, they came to a crossroads

44
00:01:42,605 --> 00:01:43,900
with many paths.

45
00:01:43,950 --> 00:01:45,832
One path began to glow.

46
00:01:45,882 --> 00:01:47,681
It pointed to the east

47
00:01:47,731 --> 00:01:49,950
the goat's lucky direction.

48
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,724
But a wide river blocked the path

49
00:01:51,774 --> 00:01:53,015
with only small stepping

50
00:01:53,065 --> 00:01:54,036
stones to cross it.

51
00:01:54,086 --> 00:01:54,950
The goat stopped.

52
00:01:55,000 --> 00:01:56,133
Sometimes, people born

53
00:01:56,183 --> 00:01:58,337
in the Year of the Goat can be indecisive

54
00:01:58,387 --> 00:01:59,950
or overly dependent on others.

55
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,546
Kong came close and smiled.

56
00:02:02,596 --> 00:02:05,046
"Breathe slowly," he said.

57
00:02:05,096 --> 00:02:06,777
"Trust your heart.

58
00:02:06,827 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

59
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,870
Stone by stone, the goat

60
00:02:11,920 --> 00:02:13,790
hopped across the river.

61
00:02:13,840 --> 00:02:16,830
Each stone glowed gold under its feet.

62
00:02:16,880 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

63
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

64
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

68
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

69
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

70
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,550
"The door opens only

71
00:02:41,600 --> 00:02:43,950
at your best time," said Kong.

72
00:02:44,000 --> 00:02:45,390
For the Goat today

73
00:02:45,440 --> 00:02:48,190
that is from 5 to 7 in the morning.

74
00:02:48,240 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,515
When the time came

76
00:02:51,565 --> 00:02:54,820
the goat rang the golden bells 7 times

77
00:02:54,870 --> 00:02:56,820
its first lucky number.

78
00:02:56,870 --> 00:02:57,863
Ding!

79
00:02:57,913 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

80
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,095
The door opened

81
00:03:01,145 --> 00:03:03,614
and a stairway of light appeared.

82
00:03:03,664 --> 00:03:06,133
The goat climbed 27 glowing steps

83
00:03:06,183 --> 00:03:07,889
its second lucky number

84
00:03:07,939 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

85
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,968
At the top of the tower

86
00:03:12,018 --> 00:03:14,687
they found the magic lotus seed

87
00:03:14,737 --> 00:03:16,792
glowing like a tiny sun.

88
00:03:16,842 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the giant lotus!

89
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,719
Around the seed, seven little lights

90
00:03:22,769 --> 00:03:25,719
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

91
00:03:25,769 --> 00:03:28,258
Friday and Sunday shone brightest

92
00:03:28,308 --> 00:03:29,950
the Goat's lucky days.

93
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,029
Time was running out.

94
00:03:32,079 --> 00:03:34,603
Holding the seed carefully

95
00:03:34,653 --> 00:03:37,376
the little hero hurried home

96
00:03:37,426 --> 00:03:39,950
with Kong right beside it.

97
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

98
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

99
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

100
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

101
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,796
The goat placed the seed

102
00:03:51,846 --> 00:03:53,258
in the giant lotus.

103
00:03:53,308 --> 00:03:55,642
For a moment, nothing happened.

104
00:03:55,692 --> 00:03:56,642
Then, whoosh!

105
00:03:56,692 --> 00:03:57,873
The lotus opened

106
00:03:57,923 --> 00:03:59,950
in a burst of golden light!

107
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,223
The giant lotus bloomed again.

108
00:04:02,273 --> 00:04:04,192
Lanterns rose into the sky

109
00:04:04,242 --> 00:04:06,692
and the full-moon festival began.

110
00:04:06,742 --> 00:04:07,905
Everyone cheered

111
00:04:07,955 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

112
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,693
The full moon shone down warmly

113
00:04:12,743 --> 00:04:14,729
as if to say thank you.

114
00:04:14,779 --> 00:04:17,649
The goat felt peaceful and proud.

115
00:04:17,699 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

116
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,256
If you were born in the Year of the Goat

117
00:04:23,306 --> 00:04:25,074
today is your day too.

118
00:04:25,124 --> 00:04:27,719
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

119
00:04:27,769 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

120
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,994
Let's remember today's luck.

121
00:04:32,044 --> 00:04:33,381
Lucky color: green.

122
00:04:33,431 --> 00:04:36,154
Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning.

123
00:04:36,204 --> 00:04:37,760
Lucky direction: east.

124
00:04:37,810 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 225.

125
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,219
And so, under the bright full moon

126
00:04:43,269 --> 00:04:46,681
the little golden hero and Kong fell

127
00:04:46,731 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

128
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

129
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

130
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

131
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

132
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

133
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Ox (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Ox, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Ox clashes with the Goat day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Ox as ingredients (the Ox is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the ox hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little candle boat keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Ox? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Ox, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Ox, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Ox? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Ox.
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗថ្ងៃនេះ គឺឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. She starts by raising one open hand toward the camera like a friendly "stop" sign, with a caring, slightly urgent expression, then lowers it and keeps talking warmly. Below her, at the edge of the pond where a path of flat stepping stones leads up to the temple, @Ox (the Ox guardian with their ox) sits looking a little tired and lets out a quiet sigh with its mouth closed; it does not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward Chanda. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the ox is the saved Flow character @Ox. Use the saved Flow character @Chanda exactly as she is: same face, hairstyle, outfit and jewelry in every scene. Her face shows quiet compassion: she holds one hand open toward the viewer as if offering comfort, and smiles reassuringly. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Sky: a gentle shower of tiny golden meteors falls far away behind the temple, each one a thin line of light; it stays in the background and never covers a face.

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 17 November, is a Goat day, opposite the Ox in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be stubborn and slow to adapt to sudden change. Pause before you react.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Goat) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Rabbit and Pig), the sign to take it easy (Ox) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Goat
2:40 Good Day: Rabbit
3:30 Good Day: Pig
4:20 Take it easy: Ox
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
★ 3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Ox? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Goat has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំមមែមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Ox? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Goat day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Goat, Rabbit and Pig are lucky today, and the Ox should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
It's Tuesday, 17 November, and the Goat has a Great Day! It's a Yin Wood day, a day of growth.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes peace, harmony, and a caring heart. Let that spirit guide you today.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Goat are often described as gentle, creative, and compassionate. A Yin Wood day!

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
This is your day. Small good choices add up to a lucky day.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Money today: A favourable day for steady income. Save a small part of today's earnings before treating yourself. A Yin Wood day!

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Wear a touch of green today: it's your lucky color. Even something small, like a ring or a phone case, counts.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Lucky direction: the east. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Face that way when you make a wish.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Your lucky numbers: 7, 27, and 225. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The Goat's lucky days of the week are Friday and Sunday. Keep them for plans that matter. A Yin Wood day!

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
A gentle reminder: Goat people can be indecisive or overly dependent on others. Stay balanced today. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Next, the Year of the Rabbit: a Good Day! The Rabbit shares a lucky triangle with the Goat. Small choices bring big luck.

A good day to meet people and start something nice. Love: Spend a little quality time with someone close. Share your luck.

លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ចំណូលនឹងនរ។ សូមសន្សំប្រាក់ចំណូលថ្ងៃនេះមួយចំណែកតូច មុននឹងចាយលើខ្លួនឯង។ ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមពាក់អ្វីមួយពណ៌បៃតង៖ វាជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក។ សូម្បីតែរបស់តូចមួយ ដូចជាចិញ្ចៀន ឬស្រោមទូរស័ព្ទ ក៏បានដែរ។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។ ពេលល្អបំផុត៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសនោះ ពេលអធិដ្ឋាន។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំពីរ ម្ភៃប្រាំពីរ និងពីររយម្ភៃប្រាំ។ សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមែ គឺថ្ងៃសុក្រ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ។ សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។ ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ជួនកាលស្ទាក់ស្ទើរក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត ឬពឹងផ្អែកលើអ្នកដទៃពេក។ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំថោះ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំមមែ។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ស្វាយ ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី រសៀល។ ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសនិរតី។ លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំមួយ សាមសិបពីរ និងបួនរយហាសិបប្រាំមួយ។ សូមញញឹម ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ប្រផេះ ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសខាងកើត។ លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំ ម្ភៃប្រាំបី និងប្រាំបីរយហាសិបប្រាំ។ សូមញញឹម ហើយចែករំលែកសំណាងជាមួយនរណាម្នាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 47 (7:40–7:50)
ឆ្នាំច៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងបី ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ទៀបភ្លឺ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 48 (7:50–8:00)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 49 (8:00–8:10)
សរុបមកវិញ៖ ឆ្នាំមមែថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ឆ្នាំថោះ និងឆ្នាំកុរថ្ងៃល្អ ហើយឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 50 (8:10–8:20)
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។ ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

Scene 51 (8:20–8:30)
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់ សូមអរគុណ៖ អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក និងអរគុណខ្លួនឯង។ ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

Scene 52 (8:30–8:40)
សូម subscribe និងតាមដាន MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។ តើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វី? ប្រាប់ខ្ញុំក្នុងមតិយោបល់ផង! សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

```
92
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,423
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ

93
00:04:12,473 --> 00:04:15,004
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ។

94
00:04:15,054 --> 00:04:18,122
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

95
00:04:18,172 --> 00:04:19,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

96
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,755
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 17 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំមមែ

97
00:04:23,805 --> 00:04:26,676
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

98
00:04:26,726 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

99
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

100
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

101
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

102
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

103
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:42,886
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលរឹងរូស

104
00:04:42,936 --> 00:04:47,473
និងយឺតក្នុងការសម្របខ្លួនទៅនឹងការផ្លាស់ប្តូរភ្លាមៗ។

105
00:04:47,523 --> 00:04:49,950
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

106
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

107
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

108
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

109
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:02,025
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

110
00:05:02,075 --> 00:05:05,139
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

111
00:05:05,189 --> 00:05:08,346
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

112
00:05:08,396 --> 00:05:09,950
សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

113
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

114
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ

115
00:05:17,143 --> 00:05:19,950
ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

116
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,364
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

117
00:05:22,414 --> 00:05:24,433
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

118
00:05:24,483 --> 00:05:27,996
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

119
00:05:28,046 --> 00:05:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

120
00:05:30,000 --> 00:05:33,446
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

121
00:05:33,496 --> 00:05:37,186
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

122
00:05:37,236 --> 00:05:38,974
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

123
00:05:39,024 --> 00:05:39,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

124

ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

125
00:05:42,692 --> 00:05:45,206
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រផេះ។

126

ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

127
00:05:50,000 --> 00:05:54,624
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

128
00:05:54,674 --> 00:05:57,015
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។

129
00:05:57,065 --> 00:05:59,950
សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

130
00:06:00,000 --> 00:06:02,713
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

131
00:06:02,763 --> 00:06:04,950
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

132
00:06:05,000 --> 00:06:09,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 យប់ ដល់ម៉ោង 1 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

133
00:06:10,000 --> 00:06:13,363
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

134
00:06:13,413 --> 00:06:17,013
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

135

ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

136
00:06:18,651 --> 00:06:19,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

137
00:06:20,000 --> 00:06:22,531

138

ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។

139

ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

140
00:06:28,172 --> 00:06:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

141
00:06:30,000 --> 00:06:30,950
ស្នេហា៖

142
00:06:31,000 --> 00:06:34,200
សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ

143

ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

144

ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

145
00:06:38,417 --> 00:06:39,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

146
00:06:40,000 --> 00:06:42,283
ឆ្នាំមមី៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

147
00:06:42,333 --> 00:06:44,394
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។

148
00:06:44,444 --> 00:06:48,061
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

149
00:06:48,111 --> 00:06:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

150
00:06:50,000 --> 00:06:53,418
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

151
00:06:53,468 --> 00:06:57,127

152

ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

154

155
00:07:02,667 --> 00:07:04,883

156
00:07:04,933 --> 00:07:09,950

157
00:07:10,000 --> 00:07:14,207
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

158
00:07:14,257 --> 00:07:16,188
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

159
00:07:16,238 --> 00:07:19,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់ ហើយរឿងល្អៗនឹងមកដល់។

160
00:07:20,000 --> 00:07:22,336
ឆ្នាំរកា៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

161
00:07:22,386 --> 00:07:24,268
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

162
00:07:24,318 --> 00:07:28,018
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

163
00:07:28,068 --> 00:07:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

164

ស្នេហា៖

165
00:07:31,017 --> 00:07:34,272
សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ

166
00:07:34,322 --> 00:07:36,984
ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

167
00:07:37,034 --> 00:07:38,509
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

168
00:07:38,559 --> 00:07:39,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

169
00:07:40,000 --> 00:07:42,109
ឆ្នាំច៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

170
00:07:42,159 --> 00:07:44,268
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។

171
00:07:44,318 --> 00:07:48,018
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

172
00:07:48,068 --> 00:07:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

173
00:07:50,000 --> 00:07:54,429
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

174
00:07:54,479 --> 00:07:56,304
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

175
00:07:56,354 --> 00:07:59,950
ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

176
00:08:00,000 --> 00:08:02,941
សរុបមកវិញ៖ ឆ្នាំមមែថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង

177
00:08:02,991 --> 00:08:05,464
ឆ្នាំថោះ និងឆ្នាំកុរថ្ងៃល្អ

178
00:08:05,514 --> 00:08:08,361
ហើយឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ គួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។

179
00:08:08,411 --> 00:08:09,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

180
00:08:10,000 --> 00:08:12,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

181
00:08:12,564 --> 00:08:16,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

182
00:08:16,496 --> 00:08:19,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

183
00:08:20,000 --> 00:08:23,371
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់ សូមអរគុណ៖

184
00:08:23,421 --> 00:08:28,371
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក និងអរគុណខ្លួនឯង។

185
00:08:28,421 --> 00:08:29,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

186
00:08:30,000 --> 00:08:32,004
សូម subscribe និងតាមដាន

187
00:08:32,054 --> 00:08:34,146
MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។

188
00:08:34,196 --> 00:08:35,843
តើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វី?

189
00:08:35,893 --> 00:08:38,254
ប្រាប់ខ្ញុំក្នុងមតិយោបល់ផង!

190
00:08:38,304 --> 00:08:39,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-17-yt8.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,473
Born in the Year of the Ox?

2
00:00:02,523 --> 00:00:04,436
Watch this before you

3
00:00:04,486 --> 00:00:06,492
do anything big today.

4
00:00:06,542 --> 00:00:09,950
Everyone else, your luck is here too.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,873
Today is a Goat day.

6
00:00:11,923 --> 00:00:14,662
Coming up: one Great Day sign

7
00:00:14,712 --> 00:00:16,873
two Good Days, one sign

8
00:00:16,923 --> 00:00:19,950
to take it easy, and all twelve.

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,514
Goat, Rabbit and Pig are lucky today

10
00:00:23,564 --> 00:00:26,287
and the Ox should slow down.

11
00:00:26,337 --> 00:00:29,950
Find your sign in the chapters below.

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,379
Before we start

13
00:00:31,429 --> 00:00:34,998
comment your sign below, and subscribe

14
00:00:35,048 --> 00:00:38,236
so you get your fortune every day.

15
00:00:38,286 --> 00:00:39,950
Keep a calm heart.

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,697
It's Tuesday, 17 November

17
00:00:42,747 --> 00:00:45,884
and the Goat has a Great Day!

18
00:00:45,934 --> 00:00:49,950
It's a Yin Wood day, a day of growth.

19
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,445
Gentle and kind, the Goat symbolizes

20
00:00:53,495 --> 00:00:56,843
peace, harmony, and a caring heart.

21
00:00:56,893 --> 00:00:59,950
Let that spirit guide you today.

22
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,080
People born in the Year

23
00:01:02,130 --> 00:01:04,950
```

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Characters (already saved in Google Flow)
Your characters are saved in Flow as @Chanda and the 12 zodiac characters named after their animals: @Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog and @Pig. For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

### Character reference image prompts
- **Chanda (ចន្ទា), the presenter (make once, use in every video):** Create a photorealistic portrait from a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists. Realistic human proportions, natural facial expression with a gentle closed-lip smile, real silk that folds and catches the light, real gold jewelry with true metallic reflections. Graceful, upright posture, one hand held open toward the temple as if presenting it, the other resting at her waist. She stands barefoot on a carved sandstone temple terrace with an ornate stone balustrade, beside a reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers; behind her, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat glows under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, with golden lanterns and soft drifting gold dust. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light on her face and hair, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, sharp focus on her eyes, ultra-detailed. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Her face is full of genuine excitement, as if sharing wonderful news with a close friend: her eyes light up, her eyebrows lift and a bright, natural smile spreads across her face as she leans slightly toward the camera. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Goat (the Goat guardian with their goat) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it dances lightly around its guardian, who paints a glowing golden mountain in the air with a giant brush, and the painting comes to life with flowers, as today's Wood magic swirls around them: fresh green leaves, glowing jade vines and young bamboo shoots that sprout and spiral through the air. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and fireflies swirl up from the reeds. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air.
